------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------

Open Instagram.

Tap on a few of your recent Reels.

Hit "View Insights."

Look at the reach breakdown =E2=80=94 followers vs. non-followers.

(ignore the numbers for now - just look at WHO you're reaching
the most)

=E2=80=8B
If most of your reach is going to your followers =E2=80=94 that's a warm
audience. They already know of you.

If most of your reach is going to non-followers =E2=80=94 that's a cold
audience. They don't know your name, your offer, or why they
should care.

Both can drive sends.

Both can grow your list.

-----------------------------------------------------------------
But what's likable and shareable to a warm audience is very
different from what's likable and shareable to a cold one. =E2=9A=A1=EF=B8=
=8F

=E2=9C=8C=EF=B8=8F to increase lead generation.

*Webinar* "From Password Risk to Digital Trust: The Case for Passkeys in Fi=
nancial Services"
*Date & Time* March 12 / 10am ET
*Speakers* Ammar Faheem, Thales and Megan Shamas, FIDO Alliance

As Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) institutions face esca=
lating identity=E2=80=91based threats, growing regulatory pressure, and ris=
ing customer expectations, authentication has become a board=E2=80=91level =
concern.

This webinar examines how FIDO=E2=80=91based authentication including passk=
eys are enabling banks, insurers, and financial institutions to reduce frau=
d, improve customer experience, and strengthen cyber resilience across thei=
r entire identity landscape.

Attendees will hear how leading banks are adopting passkeys for digital ban=
king customers and internal workforces, the business outcomes they are achi=
eving, and the critical considerations for scaling passwordless authenticat=
ion across employees, partners, and consumers.
